> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent

        Idea:
Fix 7 remaining operational pain points in the auto-improve infrastructure. These are recurring annoyances that waste time every iteration.

1. SCORER CLI: Replace ad-hoc inline Python scorer scripts with a proper CLI. Should be: python -m auto_improve.score --watch --iterations 021 022 023. Auto-discovers iterations if none specified. Survives crashes with auto-restart. Uses Modal by default. Currently we manually write 30-line nohup Python scripts every time we need scoring.

2. KILL COMMAND: Replace 'ps aux | grep | awk | xargs kill' with: python -m auto_improve.kill --iteration 021 or python -m auto_improve.kill --all. Should kill the loop process, all worker subprocesses, and the scorer. Currently we do this manually every time.

3. MANIFEST ATOMIC WRITES: Workers overwrite manifests with stale state when they die and restart. We've hit this 4-5 times causing tasks to be re-run or stuck as 'claimed' by dead workers. The manifest needs atomic writes (write to temp, rename) or file locking.

4. PROVIDER ROUTING CONSOLIDATION: _resolve_model() is copy-pasted between megaplan/hermes_worker.py and megaplan/parallel_critique.py. Extract into a single function in a shared module (e.g., megaplan/providers.py) that both import. Includes zhipu:, minimax:, google: prefixes, ~/.hermes/.env loading, and OpenRouter fallback.

5. INLINE TASK SELECTION: Replace the manual tasks.json swap dance with: python -m auto_improve.loop --tasks 20 --seed 12345 to pick N random tasks. The loop should accept --tasks N for random sampling without touching tasks.json on disk.

6. A/B COMPARISON TOOL: Add python -m auto_improve.compare iteration-021 iteration-022 that shows per-task pass/fail diffs on shared tasks, plus summary stats. Currently we eyeball check_scores output.

7. FASTER MODAL SKIP: scikit-learn and sympy repos always fail on Modal sandbox build. Auto-detect Modal sandbox failures (setup_repo.sh exit code 2, 'Error creating sandbox') and skip after 2 attempts instead of 5. Don't waste 15 min retrying something that will never work.

CONSTRAINTS:
- Don't change the megaplan pipeline (prompts, handlers, checks)
- Don't change run_evals.py phase execution logic
- Keep it simple — CLI commands, not frameworks
- Each fix should be independently useful
- Provider routing fix is in the megaplan repo at /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/megaplan

- [DEBT] search-for-related-code-that-handles-the-same-concept-is-the-reported-issue-a-symptom-of-something-broader: search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: re-checked every hermes phase that uses structured output. beyond `prep`, `critique`, `finalize`, and `review`, the same tool-plus-schema pattern still exists for `plan`, `research`, `revise`, and `gate`: toolsets are assigned in `megaplan/hermes_worker.py:51-62`, schemas are defined in `megaplan/workers.py:48-59`, and the handlers persist those worker payloads in `megaplan/handlers.py:594-780`. the revised plan intentionally keeps those phases out of scope, but that means the broader repository-level reliability problem is still not fully contained. - [DEBT] search-for-related-code-that-handles-the-same-concept-is-the-reported-issue-a-symptom-of-something-broader: search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: checked megaplan/workers.py:1022-1058, megaplan/handlers.py:679-735, megaplan/_core/state.py:95-108, and megaplan/workers.py:748-780. critique execution is not hermes-only today; it goes through generic worker dispatch, agent fallback, and one persisted `*_critic` session slot, so routing `handle_critique()` straight to `run_parallel_critique()` and `run_hermes_step()` would broaden the change into agent-routing and session-state behavior that the plan does not currently budget. - [DEBT] search-for-related-code-that-handles-the-same-concept-is-the-reported-issue-a-symptom-of-something-broader: search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: checked `megaplan/workers.py:748-783`, `megaplan/workers.py:960-1021`, and `megaplan/_core/state.py:95-108`. the revised plan fixes the earlier agent-routing problem by gating parallelism to hermes, but it still changes critique session behavior more broadly than the current repo does: `resolve_agent_mode()` defaults critique to persistent mode, while the plan assumes `fresh=true` for all sub-agents and then records the overall parallel step as `refreshed=true`, so hermes critique would stop reusing the normal `hermes_critic` session at all unless the implementation adds an explicit replacement contract. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] search-for-related-code-that-handles-the-same-concept-is-the-reported-issue-a-symptom-of-something-broader: search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: checked the broader lifecycle model around item #2 and the revised plan still does not fully contain it. the repo now needs to coordinate three process shapes: the top-level loop, per-worker process groups, and a single scorer process that may span several iterations; storing one shared `scorer_pid` inside multiple iteration-local pidfiles means the process-management problem is still broader than a per-iteration pidfile kill story. Use NNN or iteration-NNN.\"\n+    )\n+\n+\n+def load_watch_scores(results_root: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:\n+    watch_scores_path = results_root / \"_watch_scores.json\"\n+    if not watch_scores_path.exists():\n+        raise FileNotFoundError(f\"Missing watch scores: {watch_scores_path}\")\n+\n+    payload = json.loads(watch_scores_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n+    if not isinstance(payload, dict):\n+        raise ValueError(f\"Expected {watch_scores_path} to contain a JSON object\")\n+    tasks = payload.get(\"tasks\")\n+    if not isinstance(tasks, dict):\n+        raise ValueError(f\"Expected {watch_scores_path} to contain a tasks object\")\n+    return payload\n+\n+\n+def _task_outcomes(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, dict[str, str | bool]]:\n+    outcomes: dict[str, dict[str, str | bool]] = {}\n+    tasks = payload.get(\"tasks\", {})\n+    for task_id, task_payload in tasks.items():\n+        status = classify_task(task_payload if isinstance(task_payload, dict) else {})\n+        outcomes[str(task_id)] = {\n+            \"status\": status,\n+            \"passed\": status == \"pass\",\n+        }\n+    return outcomes\n+\n+\n+def compare_iterations(a_payload: dict[str, Any], b_payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:\n+    a_tasks = _task_outcomes(a_payload)\n+    b_tasks = _task_outcomes(b_payload)\n+    shared_task_ids = sorted(a_tasks.keys() & b_tasks.keys())\n+\n+    both_pass: list[str] = []\n+    both_fail: list[str] = []\n+    regressions: list[dict[str, str]] = []\n+    improvements: list[dict[str, str]] = []\n+\n+    for task_id in shared_task_ids:\n+        a_passed = bool(a_tasks[task_id][\"passed\"])\n+        b_passed = bool(b_tasks[task_id][\"passed\"])\n+        if a_passed and b_passed:\n+            both_pass.append(task_id)\n+            continue\n+        if a_passed and not b_passed:\n+            regressions.append(\n+                {\n+                    \"task_id\": task_id,\n+                    \"from_status\": str(a_tasks[task_id][\"status\"]),\n+                    \"to_status\": str(b_tasks[task_id][\"status\"]),\n+                }\n+            )\n+            continue\n+        if not a_passed and b_passed:\n+            improvements.append(\n+                {\n+                    \"task_id\": task_id,\n+                    \"from_status\": str(a_tasks[task_id][\"status\"]),\n+                    \"to_status\": str(b_tasks[task_id][\"status\"]),\n+                }\n+            )\n+            continue\n+        both_fail.append(task_id)\n+\n+    return {\n+        \"shared_task_ids\": shared_task_ids,\n+        \"both_pass\": both_pass,\n+        \"both_fail\": both_fail,\n+        \"regressions\": regressions,\n+        \"improvements\": improvements,\n+        \"net_delta\": len(improvements) - len(regressions),\n+    }\n+\n+\n+def _summary_lines(comparison: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:\n+    return [\n+        \"Summary\",\n+        f\"  shared tasks : {len(comparison['shared_task_ids'])}\",\n+        f\"  both pass    : {len(comparison['both_pass'])}\",\n+        f\"  both fail    : {len(comparison['both_fail'])}\",\n+        f\"  regressions  : {len(comparison['regressions'])}\",\n+        f\"  improvements : {len(comparison['improvements'])}\",\n+        f\"  net delta    : {comparison['net_delta']:+d}\",\n+    ]\n+\n+\n+def _change_lines(title: str, changes: list[dict[str, str]]) -> list[str]:\n+    if not changes:\n+        return [f\"{title}\", \"  none\"]\n+\n+    lines = [title]\n+    for change in changes:\n+        lines.append(\n+            f\"  {change['task_id']}: {change['from_status']} -> {change['to_status']}\"\n+        )\n+    return lines\n+\n+\n+def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n+        description=\"Compare per-task pass/fail outcomes between two auto-improve iterations.\"\n+    )\n+    parser.add_argument(\"iteration_a\", help=\"Baseline iteration: NNN or iteration-NNN.\")\n+    parser.add_argument(\"iteration_b\", help=\"Candidate iteration: NNN or iteration-NNN.\")\n+    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\n+\n+    a_root = resolve_results_root(args.iteration_a)\n+    b_root = resolve_results_root(args.iteration_b)\n+    comparison = compare_iterations(load_watch_scores(a_root), load_watch_scores(b_root))\n+\n+    lines = [\n+        f\"A: {a_root.name}\",\n+        f\"B: {b_root.name}\",\n+        \"\",\n+        *_summary_lines(comparison),\n+        \"\",\n+        *_change_lines(\"Regressions\", comparison[\"regressions\"]),\n+        \"\",\n+        *_change_lines(\"Improvements\", comparison[\"improvements\"]),\n+    ]\n+    print(\"\\n\".join(lines))\n+    return 1 if comparison[\"regressions\"] else 0\n+\n+\n+if __name__ == \"__main__\":\n+    raise SystemExit(main())\n*** End Patch\n"
}

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent

        Idea:
Fix 7 remaining operational pain points in the auto-improve infrastructure. These are recurring annoyances that waste time every iteration.

1. SCORER CLI: Replace ad-hoc inline Python scorer scripts with a proper CLI. Should be: python -m auto_improve.score --watch --iterations 021 022 023. Auto-discovers iterations if none specified. Survives crashes with auto-restart. Uses Modal by default. Currently we manually write 30-line nohup Python scripts every time we need scoring.

2. KILL COMMAND: Replace 'ps aux | grep | awk | xargs kill' with: python -m auto_improve.kill --iteration 021 or python -m auto_improve.kill --all. Should kill the loop process, all worker subprocesses, and the scorer. Currently we do this manually every time.

3. MANIFEST ATOMIC WRITES: Workers overwrite manifests with stale state when they die and restart. We've hit this 4-5 times causing tasks to be re-run or stuck as 'claimed' by dead workers. The manifest needs atomic writes (write to temp, rename) or file locking.

4. PROVIDER ROUTING CONSOLIDATION: _resolve_model() is copy-pasted between megaplan/hermes_worker.py and megaplan/parallel_critique.py. Extract into a single function in a shared module (e.g., megaplan/providers.py) that both import. Includes zhipu:, minimax:, google: prefixes, ~/.hermes/.env loading, and OpenRouter fallback.

5. INLINE TASK SELECTION: Replace the manual tasks.json swap dance with: python -m auto_improve.loop --tasks 20 --seed 12345 to pick N random tasks. The loop should accept --tasks N for random sampling without touching tasks.json on disk.

6. A/B COMPARISON TOOL: Add python -m auto_improve.compare iteration-021 iteration-022 that shows per-task pass/fail diffs on shared tasks, plus summary stats. Currently we eyeball check_scores output.

7. FASTER MODAL SKIP: scikit-learn and sympy repos always fail on Modal sandbox build. Auto-detect Modal sandbox failures (setup_repo.sh exit code 2, 'Error creating sandbox') and skip after 2 attempts instead of 5. Don't waste 15 min retrying something that will never work.

CONSTRAINTS:
- Don't change the megaplan pipeline (prompts, handlers, checks)
- Don't change run_evals.py phase execution logic
- Keep it simple — CLI commands, not frameworks
- Each fix should be independently useful
- Provider routing fix is in the megaplan repo at /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/megaplan

Workers have their own PGIDs via start_new_session=True \u2014 store both pid and pgid. Loop's own PID is os.getpid() (plain PID only, NO pgid \u2014 loop shares shell's PGID). (b) Use atomic write (temp+rename). (c) In _kill_workers() (line ~431) and at normal exit, null out loop_pid and worker entries but preserve scorer_pid. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the revised parsing approach against the real trace outputs. the repository’s saved critique responses are not plain json strings; they include prose prefixes, and at least one target trace (`django__django-10973`) wraps the json in a fenced code block. a case-insensitive substring scan over the whole response can still credit a model for mentioning a keyword anywhere in wrapper text without proving the keyword appeared in the actual critique findings, so the core comparison logic remains weaker than the requested 'extract json and inspect findings' behavior. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)

\"_pidfile.json\"\n+\n+\n+def _utc_now() -> str:\n+    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()\n+\n+\n+def _pidfile_path(results_root: Path) -> Path:\n+    return results_root / PIDFILE_NAME\n+\n+\n+def _default_pidfile_payload(iteration: str) -> dict[str, Any]:\n+    return {\n+        \"loop_pid\": None,\n+        \"workers\": [],\n+        \"scorer_pid\": None,\n+        \"started_at\": _utc_now(),\n+        \"iteration\": iteration,\n+    }\n+\n+\n+def _load_pidfile(path: Path, *, iteration: str) -> dict[str, Any]:\n+    if not path.exists():\n+        return _default_pidfile_payload(iteration)\n+\n+    payload = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n+    if not isinstance(payload, dict):\n+        raise ValueError(f\"Expected {path} to contain a JSON object\")\n+\n+    workers = payload.get(\"workers\")\n+    if not isinstance(workers, list):\n+        workers = []\n+\n+    loop_pid = payload.get(\"loop_pid\")\n+    scorer_pid = payload.get(\"scorer_pid\")\n+    started_at = payload.get(\"started_at\")\n+    payload_iteration = payload.get(\"iteration\")\n+    return {\n+        \"loop_pid\": loop_pid if isinstance(loop_pid, int) else None,\n+        \"workers\": [entry for entry in workers if isinstance(entry, dict)],\n+        \"scorer_pid\": scorer_pid if isinstance(scorer_pid, int) else None,\n+        \"started_at\": started_at if isinstance(started_at, str) and started_at.strip() else _utc_now(),\n+        \"iteration\": payload_iteration if isinstance(payload_iteration, str) and payload_iteration.strip() else iteration,\n+    }\n+\n+\n+def _write_pidfile(path: Path, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n+    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n+    with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(\n+        \"w\",\n+        encoding=\"utf-8\",\n+        dir=path.parent,\n+        prefix=\"_pidfile.\",\n+        suffix=\".tmp\",\n+        delete=False,\n+    ) as handle:\n+        json.dump(payload, handle, indent=2)\n+        handle.write(\"\\n\")\n+        temp_path = Path(handle.name)\n+    os.replace(temp_path, path)\n+\n+\n+def _worker_pid_entry(worker_id: str, proc: subprocess.Popen[Any]) -> dict[str, int | str]:\n+    try:\n+        pgid = os.getpgid(proc.pid)\n+    except (ProcessLookupError, PermissionError):\n+        pgid = proc.pid\n+    return {\n+        \"worker_id\": worker_id,\n+        \"pid\": proc.pid,\n+        \"pgid\": pgid,\n+    }\n+\n+\n+def _record_pidfile_workers(\n+    results_root: Path,\n+    worker_procs: list[tuple[str, subprocess.Popen[Any], Path]],\n+    *,\n+    loop_pid: int | None,\n+    replace_workers: bool,\n+) -> None:\n+    path = _pidfile_path(results_root)\n+    iteration = results_root.name\n+    payload = _load_pidfile(path, iteration=iteration)\n+    payload[\"iteration\"] = iteration\n+    if loop_pid is not None:\n+        payload[\"loop_pid\"] = loop_pid\n+        payload[\"started_at\"] = _utc_now()\n+\n+    new_entries = [_worker_pid_entry(worker_id, proc) for worker_id, proc, _ in worker_procs]\n+    if replace_workers:\n+        payload[\"workers\"] = new_entries\n+    else:\n+        new_worker_ids = {entry[\"worker_id\"] for entry in new_entries}\n+        existing_entries = [\n+            entry\n+            for entry in payload[\"workers\"]\n+            if isinstance(entry, dict) and entry.get(\"worker_id\") not in new_worker_ids\n+        ]\n+        payload[\"workers\"] = [*existing_entries, *new_entries]\n+\n+    _write_pidfile(path, payload)\n+\n+\n+def _remove_pidfile_workers(results_root: Path, worker_ids: list[str]) -> None:\n+    path = _pidfile_path(results_root)\n+    if not path.exists():\n+        return\n+\n+    payload = _load_pidfile(path, iteration=results_root.name)\n+    worker_id_set = set(worker_ids)\n+    payload[\"workers\"] = [\n+        entry\n+        for entry in payload[\"workers\"]\n+        if isinstance(entry, dict) and entry.get(\"worker_id\") not in worker_id_set\n+    ]\n+    _write_pidfile(path, payload)\n+\n+\n+def _clear_pidfile_runtime_state(results_root: Path) -> None:\n+    path = _pidfile_path(results_root)\n+    if not path.exists():\n+        return\n+\n+    payload = _load_pidfile(path, iteration=results_root.name)\n+    payload[\"loop_pid\"] = None\n+    payload[\"workers\"] = []\n+    _write_pidfile(path, payload)\n*** End Patch\n"
}

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent

        Idea:
Fix 7 remaining operational pain points in the auto-improve infrastructure. These are recurring annoyances that waste time every iteration.

1. SCORER CLI: Replace ad-hoc inline Python scorer scripts with a proper CLI. Should be: python -m auto_improve.score --watch --iterations 021 022 023. Auto-discovers iterations if none specified. Survives crashes with auto-restart. Uses Modal by default. Currently we manually write 30-line nohup Python scripts every time we need scoring.

2. KILL COMMAND: Replace 'ps aux | grep | awk | xargs kill' with: python -m auto_improve.kill --iteration 021 or python -m auto_improve.kill --all. Should kill the loop process, all worker subprocesses, and the scorer. Currently we do this manually every time.

3. MANIFEST ATOMIC WRITES: Workers overwrite manifests with stale state when they die and restart. We've hit this 4-5 times causing tasks to be re-run or stuck as 'claimed' by dead workers. The manifest needs atomic writes (write to temp, rename) or file locking.

4. PROVIDER ROUTING CONSOLIDATION: _resolve_model() is copy-pasted between megaplan/hermes_worker.py and megaplan/parallel_critique.py. Extract into a single function in a shared module (e.g., megaplan/providers.py) that both import. Includes zhipu:, minimax:, google: prefixes, ~/.hermes/.env loading, and OpenRouter fallback.

5. INLINE TASK SELECTION: Replace the manual tasks.json swap dance with: python -m auto_improve.loop --tasks 20 --seed 12345 to pick N random tasks. The loop should accept --tasks N for random sampling without touching tasks.json on disk.

6. A/B COMPARISON TOOL: Add python -m auto_improve.compare iteration-021 iteration-022 that shows per-task pass/fail diffs on shared tasks, plus summary stats. Currently we eyeball check_scores output.

7. FASTER MODAL SKIP: scikit-learn and sympy repos always fail on Modal sandbox build. Auto-detect Modal sandbox failures (setup_repo.sh exit code 2, 'Error creating sandbox') and skip after 2 attempts instead of 5. Don't waste 15 min retrying something that will never work.

CONSTRAINTS:
- Don't change the megaplan pipeline (prompts, handlers, checks)
- Don't change run_evals.py phase execution logic
- Keep it simple — CLI commands, not frameworks
- Each fix should be independently useful
- Provider routing fix is in the megaplan repo at /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/megaplan

Next patch threads `--skip-scoring` through `run_experiment.py`. The critical behavior is to avoid both the parallel scorer and the later normalization helpers in the same run, because those artifacts do not exist in `scoring_mode="none"`.

> TOOL

tool_use apply_patch
```json
{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/evals/parallel.py\n@@\n def _remove_pidfile_workers(results_root: Path, worker_ids: list[str]) -> None:\n@@\n     ]\n     _write_pidfile(path, payload)\n \n \n-def _clear_pidfile_runtime_state(results_root: Path) -> None:\n+def _set_pidfile_scorer_pid(results_root: Path, scorer_pid: int | None) -> None:\n+    path = _pidfile_path(results_root)\n+    payload = _load_pidfile(path, iteration=results_root.name)\n+    payload[\"scorer_pid\"] = scorer_pid\n+    _write_pidfile(path, payload)\n+\n+\n+def _clear_pidfile_runtime_state(results_root: Path, *, clear_scorer: bool = False) -> None:\n     path = _pidfile_path(results_root)\n     if not path.exists():\n         return\n \n     payload = _load_pidfile(path, iteration=results_root.name)\n     payload[\"loop_pid\"] = None\n     payload[\"workers\"] = []\n+    if clear_scorer:\n+        payload[\"scorer_pid\"] = None\n     _write_pidfile(path, payload)\n@@\n-    if scoring_mode not in {\"batch\", \"watch\"}:\n+    if scoring_mode not in {\"batch\", \"watch\", \"none\"}:\n         raise ValueError(f\"Unsupported scoring_mode: {scoring_mode}\")\n@@\n-        _wait_for_workers(worker_procs, worker_log_dir, manifest_path)\n+        worker_exit_codes = _wait_for_workers(worker_procs, worker_log_dir, manifest_path)\n@@\n-        # Clean up temp configs once worker startup is no longer needed.\n         for tc in temp_configs:\n             tc.unlink(missing_ok=True)\n \n+        if scoring_mode == \"none\":\n+            summary = {\n+                \"mode\": \"parallel\",\n+                \"workers\": workers,\n+                \"total_tasks\": len(all_tasks),\n+                \"prediction_count\": prediction_count,\n+                \"worker_exit_codes\": worker_exit_codes,\n+                \"scoring\": {\"mode\": \"none\", \"skipped\": True},\n+            }\n+            summary_path = results_root / \"summary_parallel.json\"\n+            summary_path.write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2, default=str), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n+            print(f\"\\nSummary: {summary_path}\", file=sys.stderr)\n+            return summary\n+\n         if prediction_count == 0:\n             print(\"No predictions generated — skipping scoring\", file=sys.stderr)\n             return {\"predictions\": 0, \"workers\": workers, \"tasks\": len(all_tasks)}\n*** End Patch\n"

{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/auto_improve/score.py\n+\"\"\"Watch and score active auto-improve iterations.\"\"\"\n+\n+from __future__ import annotations\n+\n+import argparse\n+import sys\n+import time\n+from datetime import datetime, timezone\n+from pathlib import Path\n+\n+from evals.parallel import _set_pidfile_scorer_pid\n+from evals.watch_scoring import (\n+    DEFAULT_POLL_INTERVAL,\n+    _score_single_prediction,\n+    _utc_now,\n+    _write_incremental_results,\n+    load_scores_data,\n+    refresh_scores_summary,\n+    write_scorer_status,\n+)\n+from evals.watch_scoring_all import discover_active_iterations, find_scorable\n+\n+\n+DEFAULT_RESULTS_BASE = Path(\"results\") / \"auto-improve\"\n+DEFAULT_RESTART_DELAY_SECONDS = 10\n+\n+\n+def _normalize_iteration_name(value: str) -> str:\n+    text = value.strip()\n+    if text.startswith(\"iteration-\"):\n+        return text\n+    if text.isdigit():\n+        return f\"iteration-{int(text):03d}\"\n+    raise ValueError(f\"Invalid iteration identifier: {value!r}\")\n+\n+\n+def _filtered_active_iterations(base_dir: Path, iteration_filters: set[str] | None) -> list[Path]:\n+    active = discover_active_iterations(base_dir)\n+    if not iteration_filters:\n+        return active\n+    return [root for root in active if root.name in iteration_filters]\n+\n+\n+def _sync_registered_roots(active_roots: list[Path], registered_roots: set[Path], scorer_pid: int) -> set[Path]:\n+    active_set = set(active_roots)\n+    for root in sorted(active_set - registered_roots):\n+        _set_pidfile_scorer_pid(root, scorer_pid)\n+        write_scorer_status(root, \"running\")\n+    for root in sorted(registered_roots - active_set):\n+        _set_pidfile_scorer_pid(root, None)\n+        write_scorer_status(root, \"idle\")\n+    return active_set\n+\n+\n+def _clear_registered_roots(registered_roots: set[Path], status: str) -> None:\n+    for root in sorted(registered_roots):\n+        _set_pidfile_scorer_pid(root, None)\n+        write_scorer_status(root, status)\n+\n+\n+def _score_iteration_root(root: Path, run_ids: dict[Path, str], *, use_modal: bool) -> bool:\n+    scores_data, scorable, _, predictions = find_scorable(root)\n+    if not scorable:\n+        return False\n+\n+    instance_id = scorable[0]\n+    run_id = run_ids.setdefault(\n+        root,\n+        f\"hermes-watch-{root.name}-{datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S%fZ')}\",\n+    )\n+    result = _score_single_prediction(\n+        predictions[instance_id],\n+        instance_id,\n+        run_id,\n+        False,\n+        use_modal=use_modal,\n+    )\n+\n+    scores_data = load_scores_data(root / \"_watch_scores.json\")\n+    previous = scores_data.get(\"tasks\", {}).get(instance_id, {})\n+    task_entry = {\n+        \"resolved\": result[\"resolved\"],\n+        \"scored_at\": _utc_now(),\n+        \"attempts\": previous.get(\"attempts\", 0) + 1 if isinstance(previous, dict) else 1,\n+    }\n+    for key in (\"error\", \"error_category\", \"returncode\"):\n+        if result.get(key) is not None:\n+            task_entry[key] = result[key]\n+    for key in (\"stderr\", \"stdout\"):\n+        if result.get(key):\n+            task_entry[key] = result[key][-1000:]\n+\n+    scores_data[\"run_id\"] = run_id\n+    scores_data.setdefault(\"tasks\", {})[instance_id] = task_entry\n+    scores_data = refresh_scores_summary(load_scores_data(root / \"_task_manifest.json\"), scores_data)\n+    _write_incremental_results(root, scores_data)\n+\n+    status = \"RESOLVED\" if result[\"resolved\"] is True else \"FAILED\" if result[\"resolved\"] is False else \"ERROR\"\n+    print(f\"[{root.name}] {instance_id}: {status}\", file=sys.stderr)\n+    return True\n+\n+\n+def _run_once(base_dir: Path, *, iteration_filters: set[str] | None, use_modal: bool) -> int:\n+    active_roots = _filtered_active_iterations(base_dir, iteration_filters)\n+    registered_roots: set[Path] = set()\n+    run_ids: dict[Path, str] = {}\n+    try:\n+        registered_roots = _sync_registered_roots(active_roots, registered_roots, os.getpid())\n+        scored = 0\n+        for root in active_roots:\n+            if _score_iteration_root(root, run_ids, use_modal=use_modal):\n+                scored += 1\n+        if not active_roots:\n+            print(\"[auto_improve.score] no active iterations found\", file=sys.stderr)\n+        return scored\n+    finally:\n+        _clear_registered_roots(registered_roots, \"idle\")\n+\n+\n+def _watch_forever(\n+    base_dir: Path,\n+    *,\n+    iteration_filters: set[str] | None,\n+    poll_interval: int,\n+    use_modal: bool,\n+) -> int:\n+    run_ids: dict[Path, str] = {}\n+    registered_roots: set[Path] = set()\n+    try:\n+        while True:\n+            active_roots = _filtered_active_iterations(base_dir, iteration_filters)\n+            registered_roots = _sync_registered_roots(active_roots, registered_roots, os.getpid())\n+            scored_any = False\n+            for root in active_roots:\n+                if _score_iteration_root(root, run_ids, use_modal=use_modal):\n+                    scored_any = True\n+            if not scored_any:\n+                time.sleep(poll_interval)\n+    finally:\n+        _clear_registered_roots(registered_roots, \"idle\")\n+\n+\n+def _parse_args(argv: list[str] | None) -> argparse.Namespace:\n+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Watch and score active auto-improve iterations.\")\n+    mode = parser.add_mutually_exclusive_group()\n+    mode.add_argument(\"--watch\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Keep watching active iterations for new predictions (default).\")\n+    mode.add_argument(\"--once\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Run a single scoring pass over the currently active iterations.\")\n+    parser.add_argument(\"--iterations\", nargs=\"+\", help=\"Optional iteration filters such as 021 022 or iteration-021.\")\n+    parser.add_argument(\"--base-dir\", default=DEFAULT_RESULTS_BASE.as_posix(), help=\"Base directory containing iteration-* results roots.\")\n+    parser.add_argument(\"--poll-interval\", type=int, default=DEFAULT_POLL_INTERVAL, help=f\"Polling interval in seconds (default: {DEFAULT_POLL_INTERVAL}).\")\n+    parser.add_argument(\"--no-modal\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Use local Docker scoring instead of Modal.\")\n+    return parser.parse_args(argv)\n+\n+\n+def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n+    import os\n+    import traceback\n+\n+    args = _parse_args(argv)\n+    base_dir = Path(args.base_dir).expanduser().resolve()\n+    iteration_filters = (\n+        {_normalize_iteration_name(value) for value in args.iterations}\n+        if args.iterations\n+        else None\n+    )\n+\n+    if args.once:\n+        _run_once(base_dir, iteration_filters=iteration_filters, use_modal=not args.no_modal)\n+        return 0\n+\n+    while True:\n+        try:\n+            return _watch_forever(\n+                base_dir,\n+                iteration_filters=iteration_filters,\n+                poll_interval=args.poll_interval,\n+                use_modal=not args.no_modal,\n+            )\n+        except KeyboardInterrupt:\n+            return 130\n+        except Exception as exc:\n+            detail = traceback.format_exc()\n+            print(\n+                \"[auto_improve.score] crash detected; restarting in \"\n+                f\"{DEFAULT_RESTART_DELAY_SECONDS}s: {exc!r}\\n{detail}\",\n+                file=sys.stderr,\n+            )\n+            time.sleep(DEFAULT_RESTART_DELAY_SECONDS)\n+\n+\n+if __name__ == \"__main__\":\n+    raise SystemExit(main())\n*** End Patch\n"

_clear_pidfile_runtime_state, _load_pidfile, _set_pidfile_scorer_pid\n+\n+\n+DEFAULT_RESULTS_BASE = Path(\"results\") / \"auto-improve\"\n+KILL_WAIT_SECONDS = 3\n+\n+\n+def _normalize_iteration_name(value: str) -> str:\n+    text = value.strip()\n+    if text.startswith(\"iteration-\"):\n+        return text\n+    if text.isdigit():\n+        return f\"iteration-{int(text):03d}\"\n+    raise ValueError(f\"Invalid iteration identifier: {value!r}\")\n+\n+\n+def _pid_alive(pid: int) -> bool:\n+    try:\n+        os.kill(pid, 0)\n+    except ProcessLookupError:\n+        return False\n+    except PermissionError:\n+        return True\n+    return True\n+\n+\n+def _pgid_alive(pgid: int) -> bool:\n+    try:\n+        os.killpg(pgid, 0)\n+    except ProcessLookupError:\n+        return False\n+    except PermissionError:\n+        return True\n+    return True\n+\n+\n+def _scan_ps_candidates(iteration_dir: Path) -> list[tuple[int, str]]:\n+    result = subprocess.run(\n+        [\"ps\", \"-axo\", \"pid=,command=\"],\n+        capture_output=True,\n+        text=True,\n+        check=False,\n+    )\n+    if result.returncode != 0:\n+        return []\n+\n+    matches: list[tuple[int, str]] = []\n+    markers = (iteration_dir.name, str(iteration_dir))\n+    for line in result.stdout.splitlines():\n+        stripped = line.strip()\n+        if not stripped:\n+            continue\n+        try:\n+            pid_text, command = stripped.split(None, 1)\n+        except ValueError:\n+            continue\n+        if not any(marker in command for marker in markers):\n+            continue\n+        if not any(token in command for token in (\"auto_improve.loop\", \"auto_improve.score\", \"evals.run_evals\")):\n+            continue\n+        try:\n+            matches.append((int(pid_text), command))\n+        except ValueError:\n+            continue\n+    return matches\n+\n+\n+def _signal_pid(pid: int, sig: signal.Signals) -> None:\n+    os.kill(pid, sig)\n+\n+\n+def _signal_pgid(pgid: int, sig: signal.Signals) -> None:\n+    os.killpg(pgid, sig)\n+\n+\n+def _iteration_dir(base_dir: Path, iteration_name: str) -> Path:\n+    return base_dir / iteration_name\n+\n+\n+def _collect_iteration_dirs(base_dir: Path, args: argparse.Namespace) -> list[Path]:\n+    if args.all:\n+        return sorted(path for path in base_dir.glob(\"iteration-*\") if path.is_dir())\n+    return [_iteration_dir(base_dir, _normalize_iteration_name(args.iteration))]\n+\n+\n+def _kill_from_pidfile(iteration_dir: Path, *, dry_run: bool) -> tuple[bool, bool]:\n+    pidfile = iteration_dir / \"_pidfile.json\"\n+    payload = _load_pidfile(pidfile, iteration=iteration_dir.name)\n+    loop_pid = payload.get(\"loop_pid\")\n+    scorer_pid = payload.get(\"scorer_pid\")\n+    workers = payload.get(\"workers\", [])\n+    killed_scorer = False\n+    saw_targets = False\n+\n+    if isinstance(loop_pid, int) and _pid_alive(loop_pid):\n+        saw_targets = True\n+        print(f\"[{iteration_dir.name}] loop pid={loop_pid} via os.kill()\", file=sys.stderr)\n+        if not dry_run:\n+            _signal_pid(loop_pid, signal.SIGTERM)\n+    for worker in workers:\n+        pgid = worker.get(\"pgid\")\n+        worker_id = worker.get(\"worker_id\", \"worker\")\n+        if not isinstance(pgid, int) or not _pgid_alive(pgid):\n+            continue\n+        saw_targets = True\n+        print(f\"[{iteration_dir.name}] worker {worker_id} pgid={pgid} via os.killpg()\", file=sys.stderr)\n+        if not dry_run:\n+            _signal_pgid(pgid, signal.SIGTERM)\n+    if isinstance(scorer_pid, int) and _pid_alive(scorer_pid):\n+        saw_targets = True\n+        killed_scorer = True\n+        print(\n+            f\"[{iteration_dir.name}] scorer pid={scorer_pid} via os.kill() \"\n+            \"(warning: this scorer may be shared with other iterations)\",\n+            file=sys.stderr,\n+        )\n+        if not dry_run:\n+            _signal_pid(scorer_pid, signal.SIGTERM)\n+\n+    if not dry_run and saw_targets:\n+        time.sleep(KILL_WAIT_SECONDS)\n+        if isinstance(loop_pid, int) and _pid_alive(loop_pid):\n+            _signal_pid(loop_pid, signal.SIGKILL)\n+        for worker in workers:\n+            pgid = worker.get(\"pgid\")\n+            if isinstance(pgid, int) and _pgid_alive(pgid):\n+                _signal_pgid(pgid, signal.SIGKILL)\n+        if isinstance(scorer_pid, int) and _pid_alive(scorer_pid):\n+            _signal_pid(scorer_pid, signal.SIGKILL)\n+\n+    if not dry_run:\n+        _clear_pidfile_runtime_state(iteration_dir, clear_scorer=killed_scorer)\n+\n+    return saw_targets, killed_scorer\n+\n+\n+def _kill_from_ps_scan(iteration_dir: Path, *, dry_run: bool) -> bool:\n+    matches = _scan_ps_candidates(iteration_dir)\n+    if not matches:\n+        print(f\"[{iteration_dir.name}] no pidfile and no matching ps candidates found\", file=sys.stderr)\n+        return False\n+\n+    print(\n+        f\"[{iteration_dir.name}] warning: pidfile missing, falling back to imprecise ps scan\",\n+        file=sys.stderr,\n+    )\n+    for pid, command in matches:\n+        print(f\"[{iteration_dir.name}] fallback pid={pid} command={command}\", file=sys.stderr)\n+        if not dry_run and _pid_alive(pid):\n+            _signal_pid(pid, signal.SIGTERM)\n+    if not dry_run:\n+        time.sleep(KILL_WAIT_SECONDS)\n+        for pid, _ in matches:\n+            if _pid_alive(pid):\n+                _signal_pid(pid, signal.SIGKILL)\n+    return True\n+\n+\n+def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Terminate auto-improve loop, worker, and scorer processes.\")\n+    target = parser.add_mutually_exclusive_group(required=True)\n+    target.add_argument(\"--iteration\", help=\"Iteration to stop, such as 021 or iteration-021.\")\n+    target.add_argument(\"--all\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Stop every iteration with a pidfile under results/auto-improve.\")\n+    parser.add_argument(\"--base-dir\", default=DEFAULT_RESULTS_BASE.as_posix(), help=\"Base directory containing iteration-* result directories.\")\n+    parser.add_argument(\"--dry-run\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Show what would be killed without sending any signals.\")\n+    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\n+\n+    base_dir = Path(args.base_dir).expanduser().resolve()\n+    iteration_dirs = _collect_iteration_dirs(base_dir, args)\n+    killed_any = False\n+    scorer_clears: list[Path] = []\n+\n+    for iteration_dir in iteration_dirs:\n+        pidfile = iteration_dir / \"_pidfile.json\"\n+        if pidfile.exists():\n+            saw_targets, killed_scorer = _kill_from_pidfile(iteration_dir, dry_run=args.dry_run)\n+            killed_any = killed_any or saw_targets\n+            if killed_scorer and not args.dry_run:\n+                scorer_clears.append(iteration_dir)\n+            continue\n+        killed_any = _kill_from_ps_scan(iteration_dir, dry_run=args.dry_run) or killed_any\n+\n+    if not args.dry_run and scorer_clears:\n+        for iteration_dir in iteration_dirs:\n+            pidfile = iteration_dir / \"_pidfile.json\"\n+            if not pidfile.exists():\n+                continue\n+            payload = _load_pidfile(pidfile, iteration=iteration_dir.name)\n+            if isinstance(payload.get(\"scorer_pid\"), int):\n+                _set_pidfile_scorer_pid(iteration_dir, None)\n+\n+    if not killed_any:\n+        print(\"No live loop, worker, or scorer processes found.\", file=sys.stderr)\n+    return 0\n+\n+\n+if __name__ == \"__main__\":\n+    raise SystemExit(main())\n*** End Patch\n"
}

evals.parallel import _set_pidfile_scorer_pid\n+from evals.watch_scoring import (\n+    DEFAULT_POLL_INTERVAL,\n+    _score_single_prediction,\n+    _utc_now,\n+    _write_incremental_results,\n+    load_scores_data,\n+    refresh_scores_summary,\n+    write_scorer_status,\n+)\n+from evals.watch_scoring_all import discover_active_iterations, find_scorable\n+\n+\n+DEFAULT_RESULTS_BASE = Path(\"results\") / \"auto-improve\"\n+DEFAULT_RESTART_DELAY_SECONDS = 10\n+\n+\n+def _normalize_iteration_name(value: str) -> str:\n+    text = value.strip()\n+    if text.startswith(\"iteration-\"):\n+        return text\n+    if text.isdigit():\n+        return f\"iteration-{int(text):03d}\"\n+    raise ValueError(f\"Invalid iteration identifier: {value!r}\")\n+\n+\n+def _filtered_active_iterations(base_dir: Path, iteration_filters: set[str] | None) -> list[Path]:\n+    active = discover_active_iterations(base_dir)\n+    if not iteration_filters:\n+        return active\n+    return [root for root in active if root.name in iteration_filters]\n+\n+\n+def _sync_registered_roots(active_roots: list[Path], registered_roots: set[Path], scorer_pid: int) -> set[Path]:\n+    active_set = set(active_roots)\n+    for root in sorted(active_set - registered_roots):\n+        _set_pidfile_scorer_pid(root, scorer_pid)\n+        write_scorer_status(root, \"running\")\n+    for root in sorted(registered_roots - active_set):\n+        _set_pidfile_scorer_pid(root, None)\n+        write_scorer_status(root, \"idle\")\n+    return active_set\n+\n+\n+def _clear_registered_roots(registered_roots: set[Path], status: str) -> None:\n+    for root in sorted(registered_roots):\n+        _set_pidfile_scorer_pid(root, None)\n+        write_scorer_status(root, status)\n+\n+\n+def _score_iteration_root(root: Path, run_ids: dict[Path, str], *, use_modal: bool) -> bool:\n+    scores_data, scorable, _, predictions = find_scorable(root)\n+    if not scorable:\n+        return False\n+\n+    instance_id = scorable[0]\n+    run_id = run_ids.setdefault(\n+        root,\n+        f\"hermes-watch-{root.name}-{datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S%fZ')}\",\n+    )\n+    result = _score_single_prediction(\n+        predictions[instance_id],\n+        instance_id,\n+        run_id,\n+        False,\n+        use_modal=use_modal,\n+    )\n+\n+    scores_data = load_scores_data(root / \"_watch_scores.json\")\n+    previous = scores_data.get(\"tasks\", {}).get(instance_id, {})\n+    task_entry = {\n+        \"resolved\": result[\"resolved\"],\n+        \"scored_at\": _utc_now(),\n+        \"attempts\": previous.get(\"attempts\", 0) + 1 if isinstance(previous, dict) else 1,\n+    }\n+    for key in (\"error\", \"error_category\", \"returncode\"):\n+        if result.get(key) is not None:\n+            task_entry[key] = result[key]\n+    for key in (\"stderr\", \"stdout\"):\n+        if result.get(key):\n+            task_entry[key] = result[key][-1000:]\n+\n+    scores_data[\"run_id\"] = run_id\n+    scores_data.setdefault(\"tasks\", {})[instance_id] = task_entry\n+    scores_data = refresh_scores_summary(TaskManifest.load(root / \"_task_manifest.json\"), scores_data)\n+    _write_incremental_results(root, scores_data)\n+\n+    status = \"RESOLVED\" if result[\"resolved\"] is True else \"FAILED\" if result[\"resolved\"] is False else \"ERROR\"\n+    print(f\"[{root.name}] {instance_id}: {status}\", file=sys.stderr)\n+    return True\n+\n+\n+def _run_once(base_dir: Path, *, iteration_filters: set[str] | None, use_modal: bool) -> int:\n+    active_roots = _filtered_active_iterations(base_dir, iteration_filters)\n+    registered_roots: set[Path] = set()\n+    run_ids: dict[Path, str] = {}\n+    try:\n+        registered_roots = _sync_registered_roots(active_roots, registered_roots, os.getpid())\n+        scored = 0\n+        for root in active_roots:\n+            if _score_iteration_root(root, run_ids, use_modal=use_modal):\n+                scored += 1\n+        if not active_roots:\n+            print(\"[auto_improve.score] no active iterations found\", file=sys.stderr)\n+        return scored\n+    finally:\n+        _clear_registered_roots(registered_roots, \"idle\")\n+\n+\n+def _watch_forever(\n+    base_dir: Path,\n+    *,\n+    iteration_filters: set[str] | None,\n+    poll_interval: int,\n+    use_modal: bool,\n+) -> int:\n+    run_ids: dict[Path, str] = {}\n+    registered_roots: set[Path] = set()\n+    try:\n+        while True:\n+            active_roots = _filtered_active_iterations(base_dir, iteration_filters)\n+            registered_roots = _sync_registered_roots(active_roots, registered_roots, os.getpid())\n+            scored_any = False\n+            for root in active_roots:\n+                if _score_iteration_root(root, run_ids, use_modal=use_modal):\n+                    scored_any = True\n+            if not scored_any:\n+                time.sleep(poll_interval)\n+    finally:\n+        _clear_registered_roots(registered_roots, \"idle\")\n+\n+\n+def _parse_args(argv: list[str] | None) -> argparse.Namespace:\n+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Watch and score active auto-improve iterations.\")\n+    mode = parser.add_mutually_exclusive_group()\n+    mode.add_argument(\"--watch\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Keep watching active iterations for new predictions (default).\")\n+    mode.add_argument(\"--once\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Run a single scoring pass over the currently active iterations.\")\n+    parser.add_argument(\"--iterations\", nargs=\"+\", help=\"Optional iteration filters such as 021 022 or iteration-021.\")\n+    parser.add_argument(\"--base-dir\", default=DEFAULT_RESULTS_BASE.as_posix(), help=\"Base directory containing iteration-* results roots.\")\n+    parser.add_argument(\"--poll-interval\", type=int, default=DEFAULT_POLL_INTERVAL, help=f\"Polling interval in seconds (default: {DEFAULT_POLL_INTERVAL}).\")\n+    parser.add_argument(\"--no-modal\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Use local Docker scoring instead of Modal.\")\n+    return parser.parse_args(argv)\n+\n+\n+def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n+    import traceback\n+\n+    args = _parse_args(argv)\n+    base_dir = Path(args.base_dir).expanduser().resolve()\n+    iteration_filters = (\n+        {_normalize_iteration_name(value) for value in args.iterations}\n+        if args.iterations\n+        else None\n+    )\n+\n+    if args.once:\n+        _run_once(base_dir, iteration_filters=iteration_filters, use_modal=not args.no_modal)\n+        return 0\n+\n+    while True:\n+        try:\n+            return _watch_forever(\n+                base_dir,\n+                iteration_filters=iteration_filters,\n+                poll_interval=args.poll_interval,\n+                use_modal=not args.no_modal,\n+            )\n+        except KeyboardInterrupt:\n+            return 130\n+        except Exception as exc:\n+            detail = traceback.format_exc()\n+            print(\n+                \"[auto_improve.score] crash detected; restarting in \"\n+                f\"{DEFAULT_RESTART_DELAY_SECONDS}s: {exc!r}\\n{detail}\",\n+                file=sys.stderr,\n+            )\n+            time.sleep(DEFAULT_RESTART_DELAY_SECONDS)\n+\n+\n+if __name__ == \"__main__\":\n+    raise SystemExit(main())\n*** End Patch\n"
}

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent

        Idea:
Fix 7 remaining operational pain points in the auto-improve infrastructure. These are recurring annoyances that waste time every iteration.

1. SCORER CLI: Replace ad-hoc inline Python scorer scripts with a proper CLI. Should be: python -m auto_improve.score --watch --iterations 021 022 023. Auto-discovers iterations if none specified. Survives crashes with auto-restart. Uses Modal by default. Currently we manually write 30-line nohup Python scripts every time we need scoring.

2. KILL COMMAND: Replace 'ps aux | grep | awk | xargs kill' with: python -m auto_improve.kill --iteration 021 or python -m auto_improve.kill --all. Should kill the loop process, all worker subprocesses, and the scorer. Currently we do this manually every time.

3. MANIFEST ATOMIC WRITES: Workers overwrite manifests with stale state when they die and restart. We've hit this 4-5 times causing tasks to be re-run or stuck as 'claimed' by dead workers. The manifest needs atomic writes (write to temp, rename) or file locking.

4. PROVIDER ROUTING CONSOLIDATION: _resolve_model() is copy-pasted between megaplan/hermes_worker.py and megaplan/parallel_critique.py. Extract into a single function in a shared module (e.g., megaplan/providers.py) that both import. Includes zhipu:, minimax:, google: prefixes, ~/.hermes/.env loading, and OpenRouter fallback.

5. INLINE TASK SELECTION: Replace the manual tasks.json swap dance with: python -m auto_improve.loop --tasks 20 --seed 12345 to pick N random tasks. The loop should accept --tasks N for random sampling without touching tasks.json on disk.

6. A/B COMPARISON TOOL: Add python -m auto_improve.compare iteration-021 iteration-022 that shows per-task pass/fail diffs on shared tasks, plus summary stats. Currently we eyeball check_scores output.

7. FASTER MODAL SKIP: scikit-learn and sympy repos always fail on Modal sandbox build. Auto-detect Modal sandbox failures (setup_repo.sh exit code 2, 'Error creating sandbox') and skip after 2 attempts instead of 5. Don't waste 15 min retrying something that will never work.

CONSTRAINTS:
- Don't change the megaplan pipeline (prompts, handlers, checks)
- Don't change run_evals.py phase execution logic
- Keep it simple — CLI commands, not frameworks
- Each fix should be independently useful
- Provider routing fix is in the megaplan repo at /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/megaplan
